267: FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::FLaneContour FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::BuildLaneContour(double MaxSquareDistanceFromCap) const
268: {
269: 	// PURE: it samples the spline and reads the layout, and touches neither the arrangement nor Owner's mutable
270: 	// state. Nothing here can half-build the polygon, because nothing here is stored on it.
271: 	FLaneContour Out;
272: 
273: 	const auto& RoadSplineCache = GetRoadSpline();
274: 	const auto& Section = GetSection();
275: 	const FRoadZoneSidewalk* AsSidewalk = GetRoadZone().GetPtr<FRoadZoneSidewalk>();
276: 
277: 	// Taken from the INNER side because it is the one every lane has (the centre line included).
278: 	const TArray<FRoadPosition> InsideLinePositions = GetPolyline(ERoadLaneSide::Inner);
279: 	Out.Inside = GetPolyline2D(InsideLinePositions);
280: 
281: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
282: 	{
283: 		Out.Outside = GetPolyline2D(GetPolyline(ERoadLaneSide::Outer));
284: 	}
285: 
286: 	if (AsSidewalk && LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
287: 	{
288: 		const FRoadLane& Lane = GetLane();
289: 
290: 		if (AsSidewalk->bEndCurb && AsSidewalk->EndCapCurve.GetRichCurveConst()->GetNumKeys())
291: 		{
292: 			double LaneWidth = Lane.Width.Eval(Lane.GetEndOffset());
293: 
294: 			TArray<float> Values;
295: 			TArray<float> Times;
296: 			CurveUtils::CurveToPolyline(*AsSidewalk->EndCapCurve.GetRichCurveConst(), 0.0, 1.0, MaxSquareDistanceFromCap / (LaneWidth * LaneWidth), 0.0001f, 200, Values, Times);
297: 			double MaxValue = *Algo::MaxElement(Values);
298: 
299: 			for (int i = 0; i < Values.Num(); ++i)
300: 			{
301: 				Out.EndCap.Add(FVector2D{ RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, LaneIndex, Times[i], Lane.GetEndOffset() - (MaxValue - Values[i]) * LaneWidth, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World).Location});
302: 			}
303: 			if (MaxValue > 0)
304: 			{
305: 				RemovePointsFromEnd(Out.Inside, MaxValue * LaneWidth);
306: 				RemovePointsFromEnd(Out.Outside, MaxValue * LaneWidth);
307: 
308: 				Out.Inside.Add(Out.EndCap[0]);
309: 				Out.Outside.Add(Out.EndCap.Last());
310: 			}
311: 		}
312: 		if (AsSidewalk->bBeginCurb && AsSidewalk->BeginCapCurve.GetRichCurveConst()->GetNumKeys())
313: 		{
314: 			double LaneWidth = Lane.Width.Eval(Section.SOffset);
315: 
316: 			TArray<float> Values;
317: 			TArray<float> Times;
318: 			CurveUtils::CurveToPolyline(*AsSidewalk->BeginCapCurve.GetRichCurveConst(), 0.0, 1.0, MaxSquareDistanceFromCap / (LaneWidth * LaneWidth), 0.0001f, 200, Values, Times);
319: 			double MaxValue = *Algo::MaxElement(Values);
320: 
321: 			for (int i = 0; i < Values.Num(); ++i)
322: 			{
323: 				Out.BeginCap.Add(FVector2D{ RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, LaneIndex, Times[i], Section.SOffset + (MaxValue - Values[i]) * LaneWidth, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World).Location });
324: 			}
325: 			if (MaxValue > 0)
326: 			{
327: 				RemovePointsFromBegin(Out.Inside, MaxValue * LaneWidth);
328: 				RemovePointsFromBegin(Out.Outside, MaxValue * LaneWidth);
329: 
330: 				Out.Inside.Insert(Out.BeginCap[0], 0);
331: 				Out.Outside.Insert(Out.BeginCap.Last(), 0);
332: 			}
333: 		}
334: 	}
335: 
336: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
337: 	{
338: 		if (Out.BeginCap.Num() < 2)
339: 		{
340: 			Out.BeginCap.Empty();
341: 			Out.BeginCap.Add(Out.Inside[0]);
342: 			Out.BeginCap.Add(Out.Outside[0]);
---
408: 	if (!BeginCapInfo.IsValid())
409: 	{
410: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_BeginCapInfo", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: BeginCapInfo line info failed "), GetDescription()));
411: 		return false;
412: 	}
413: 
414: 	if (!EndCapInfo.IsValid())
415: 	{
416: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_EndCapInfo", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: EndCapInfo line info failed "), GetDescription()));
417: 		return false;
418: 	}
419: 
420: 	// Folded into this phase rather than given one of its own: the seams' only ordering constraint is "after both
421: 	// boundary polylines are final", which is here. A separate phase would add the rule "immediately after the
422: 	// second one" and buy nothing.
423: 	InsertZoneBoundaryEdges(Builder, Contour.Inside, Contour.Outside);
424: 
425: 	return true;
426: }
427: 
428: void FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::RunLaneAttributeHooks(FRoadArrangementBuilder& Builder)
429: {
430: 	// LAST, on a COMPLETE polygon: the hooks read this polygon's geometry, so they cannot run before the contour
431: 	// is inserted. Builder -- not *this* -- is the sink now; the polygon is the subject only.
432: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
433: 	{
434: 		for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : GetLane().Attributes)
435: 		{
436: 			if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
437: 			{
438: 				for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
439: 				{
440: 					if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
441: 					{
442: 						Value->OnPolygonCreated(*this, Builder, Key.SOffset);
443: 					}
444: 				}
445: 			}
446: 		}
447: 	}
448: 
449: #if METAROAD_PRO
450: 	// The centre line takes part in this one (its attributes live on the section), which is why it sits outside
451: 	// the lane-only block above rather than beside the hook it looks like.
452: 	for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : GetLaneAttributes())
453: 	{
454: 		if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonValue>())
455: 		{
456: 			for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
457: 			{
458: 				if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonValue>())
459: 				{
460: 					const FRoadPosition RoadPose = GetRoadSpline().EvalAttributeAnchorPosition(SectionIndex, LaneIndex, GetStartOffset() + Key.SOffset, *Value, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World);
461: 					const bool bIsForward = (LaneIndex == MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex) || GetRoadSpline().GetRoadLayout().Sections[SectionIndex].GetLaneByIndex(LaneIndex).IsForwardLane();
462: 					const FTransform Transform(RoadPose.Quat * FRotator(0.0, bIsForward ? -90.0 : +90.0, 0.0).Quaternion(), RoadPose.Location, FVector(-1.0, 1.0, 1.0));
463: 					// Appends to the build's working fill list, in lane order -- which is what fixes
464: 					// FProceduralPolygon_Simple's index and therefore GetObjectIndex().
465: 					Builder.AddSurfacePolygons(Value->BuildPolygons(Transform));
466: 				}
467: 			}
468: 		}
469: 	}
470: #endif
471: }
472: 
473: 
474: const FRoadLaneSection& FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetSection() const
475: {
476: 	return GetRoadSpline().RoadLayout.Sections[SectionIndex];
477: }
478: 
479: const FRoadLane& FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetLane() const
480: {
481: 	check(LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex);
482: 	if (LaneIndex > 0)
483: 	{
484: 		return GetSection().Right[LaneIndex - 1];
485: 	}
486: 	else
487: 	{
488: 		return GetSection().Left[-LaneIndex - 1];
489: 	}
490: }
491: 
492: const TMap<TSoftClassPtr<URoadLaneAttributeDescriptor>, FRoadLaneAttribute>& FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetLaneAttributes() const
493: {
494: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
495: 	{
496: 		return GetLane().Attributes;
497: 	}
498: 	else
499: 	{
500: 		return GetSection().Attributes;
501: 	}
502: }
503: 
504: double FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetStartOffset() const
505: {
506: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
507: 	{
508: 		return GetLane().GetStartOffset();
---
414: 	if (!EndCapInfo.IsValid())
415: 	{
416: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_EndCapInfo", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: EndCapInfo line info failed "), GetDescription()));
417: 		return false;
418: 	}
419: 
420: 	// Folded into this phase rather than given one of its own: the seams' only ordering constraint is "after both
421: 	// boundary polylines are final", which is here. A separate phase would add the rule "immediately after the
422: 	// second one" and buy nothing.
423: 	InsertZoneBoundaryEdges(Builder, Contour.Inside, Contour.Outside);
424: 
425: 	return true;
426: }

496: 		return GetLane().Attributes;
497: 	}
498: 	else
499: 	{
500: 		return GetSection().Attributes;
501: 	}
502: }
503: 
504: double FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetStartOffset() const
505: {
506: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
507: 	{
508: 		return GetLane().GetStartOffset();
509: 	}
510: 	else
511: 	{
512: 		return GetSection().SOffset;
513: 	}
514: }

517: {
518: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
519: 	{
520: 		return GetLane().GetEndOffset();
521: 	}
522: 	else
523: 	{
524: 		return GetSection().SOffsetEnd_Cached;
525: 	}
526: }
527: 
528: bool FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::OnCompleteArrangement(FRoadCompletionSink& Sink)
529: {
530: 	if (ResultInfo.HasFailed())
---
432: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
433: 	{
434: 		for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : GetLane().Attributes)
435: 		{
436: 			if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
437: 			{
438: 				for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
439: 				{
440: 					if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
441: 					{
442: 						Value->OnPolygonCreated(*this, Builder, Key.SOffset);
443: 					}
444: 				}
445: 			}
446: 		}
447: 	}
448: 
449: #if METAROAD_PRO
450: 	// The centre line takes part in this one (its attributes live on the section), which is why it sits outside
451: 	// the lane-only block above rather than beside the hook it looks like.
452: 	for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : GetLaneAttributes())
453: 	{
454: 		if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonValue>())
455: 		{
456: 			for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
457: 			{
458: 				if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonValue>())
459: 				{
460: 					const FRoadPosition RoadPose = GetRoadSpline().EvalAttributeAnchorPosition(SectionIndex, LaneIndex, GetStartOffset() + Key.SOffset, *Value, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World);
461: 					const bool bIsForward = (LaneIndex == MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex) || GetRoadSpline().GetRoadLayout().Sections[SectionIndex].GetLaneByIndex(LaneIndex).IsForwardLane();
462: 					const FTransform Transform(RoadPose.Quat * FRotator(0.0, bIsForward ? -90.0 : +90.0, 0.0).Quaternion(), RoadPose.Location, FVector(-1.0, 1.0, 1.0));
463: 					// Appends to the build's working fill list, in lane order -- which is what fixes
464: 					// FProceduralPolygon_Simple's index and therefore GetObjectIndex().
465: 					Builder.AddSurfacePolygons(Value->BuildPolygons(Transform));
466: 				}
467: 			}
468: 		}
469: 	}
470: #endif
471: }

538: 	{
539: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_InsideLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: Inside line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
540: 		return false;

515: 
516: double FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetEndOffset() const
517: {
518: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
519: 	{
520: 		return GetLane().GetEndOffset();
521: 	}
522: 	else
523: 	{
524: 		return GetSection().SOffsetEnd_Cached;
525: 	}
526: }
527: 
528: bool FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::OnCompleteArrangement(FRoadCompletionSink& Sink)
529: {
530: 	if (ResultInfo.HasFailed())
531: 	{
532: 		return false;
533: 	}
534: 
535: 	FRoadVertexTable& Vertices = Sink.Vertices;
536: 
537: 	if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, InsideLineInfo, InsideLineVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
538: 	{
539: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_InsideLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: Inside line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
540: 		return false;
541: 	}
542: 
543: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
544: 	{
545: 		if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, OutsideLineInfo, OutsideLineVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
546: 		{
547: 			ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_OutsideLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: Outside line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
548: 			return false;
549: 		}
550: 
551: 		if (!bIsLoop)
552: 		{
553: 			if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, BeginCapInfo, BeginCapVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
554: 			{
555: 				ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_BeginCapeNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: BeginCap line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
556: 				return false;
557: 			}
558: 
559: 			if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, EndCapInfo, EndCapVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
560: 			{
561: 				ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_EndCapLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: EndCap line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
562: 				return false;
563: 			}
564: 		}
565: 		else
566: 		{
567: 			if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, BeginCapInfo, BeginCapVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ true))
568: 			{
569: 				ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_LoopSeamNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop: {0}: LoopSeam line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
570: 				return false;

476: 	return GetRoadSpline().RoadLayout.Sections[SectionIndex];

488: 		return GetSection().Left[-LaneIndex - 1];
489: 	}

528: bool FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::OnCompleteArrangement(FRoadCompletionSink& Sink)
529: {
530: 	if (ResultInfo.HasFailed())
531: 	{
532: 		return false;
533: 	}
534: 
535: 	FRoadVertexTable& Vertices = Sink.Vertices;
536: 
537: 	if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, InsideLineInfo, InsideLineVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
538: 	{
539: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_InsideLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: Inside line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
540: 		return false;
541: 	}
542: 
543: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
544: 	{
545: 		if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, OutsideLineInfo, OutsideLineVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
546: 		{
547: 			ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_OutsideLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: Outside line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
548: 			return false;
549: 		}
550: 
551: 		if (!bIsLoop)
552: 		{
553: 			if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, BeginCapInfo, BeginCapVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
554: 			{
555: 				ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_BeginCapeNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: BeginCap line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
556: 				return false;
557: 			}
558: 
559: 			if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, EndCapInfo, EndCapVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
560: 			{
561: 				ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_EndCapLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: EndCap line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
562: 				return false;
563: 			}
564: 		}
565: 		else
566: 		{
567: 			if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, BeginCapInfo, BeginCapVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ true))
568: 			{
569: 				ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_LoopSeamNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop: {0}: LoopSeam line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
570: 				return false;
571: 			}
572: 		}
573: 	}
574: 
575: 	if (LaneIndex == MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
576: 	{
---
480: {
481: 	check(LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex);
482: 	if (LaneIndex > 0)
483: 	{
484: 		return GetSection().Right[LaneIndex - 1];
485: 	}
486: 	else
487: 	{
488: 		return GetSection().Left[-LaneIndex - 1];
489: 	}
490: }

493: {

495: 	{

497: 	}
498: 	else
499: 	{
500: 		return GetSection().Attributes;
501: 	}

517: {
518: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
519: 	{

521: 	}

523: 	{
524: 		return GetSection().SOffsetEnd_Cached;

527: 
528: bool FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::OnCompleteArrangement(FRoadCompletionSink& Sink)
529: {
530: 	if (ResultInfo.HasFailed())
531: 	{
532: 		return false;
533: 	}
534: 
535: 	FRoadVertexTable& Vertices = Sink.Vertices;
536: 
537: 	if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, InsideLineInfo, InsideLineVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
538: 	{
539: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_InsideLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: Inside line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
540: 		return false;
541: 	}
542: 
543: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
544: 	{
545: 		if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, OutsideLineInfo, OutsideLineVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
546: 		{
547: 			ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_OutsideLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: Outside line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
548: 			return false;
549: 		}
550: 
551: 		if (!bIsLoop)
552: 		{
553: 			if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, BeginCapInfo, BeginCapVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
554: 			{
555: 				ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_BeginCapeNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: BeginCap line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
556: 				return false;
557: 			}
558: 
559: 			if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, EndCapInfo, EndCapVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ false))
560: 			{
561: 				ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_EndCapLineNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane: {0}: EndCap line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
562: 				return false;
563: 			}
564: 		}
565: 		else
566: 		{
567: 			if (!ProcessPolyline(Vertices, BeginCapInfo, BeginCapVertices, /*bLoopSeam*/ true))
568: 			{
569: 				ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_LoopSeamNotFound", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop: {0}: LoopSeam line not found after arrangement"), GetDescription()));
570: 				return false;
571: 			}
572: 		}
573: 	}
574: 
575: 	if (LaneIndex == MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
576: 	{
577: 		for (int i = 0; i < InsideLineVertices.Num() - 1; ++i)
578: 		{
579: 			Boundary.Add({ InsideLineVertices[i], InsideLineVertices[i + 1] });
580: 		}
---
742: 	VertexInfo.Pos = Pos;
743: 	VertexInfo.Pos.Location.X = V2d.X;
744: 	VertexInfo.Pos.Location.Y = V2d.Y;
745: 	VertexInfo.Alpha0 = NormalizeLateral(Pos.ROffset - ROffset1, ROffset2 - ROffset1);  // Alpha0 = [0..1]
746: 	VertexInfo.Alpha1 = bWithUV1 ? NormalizeLateral(VertexInfo.Pos.ROffset - SplineBounds.Max.Y, RoadWidth) : 0.0; // Alpha1 = [0..1]
747: 	VertexInfo.VID = VID;
748: 	VertexInfo.bLoopSeam = bLoopSeam;
749: 	VertexInfo.HeightOffset = 0;
750: 
751: 	if (const FRoadZoneDriving* LaneDriving = GetRoadZone().GetPtr<FRoadZoneDriving>())
752: 	{
753: 		if (LaneDriving && LaneDriving->bInvertUV0)
754: 		{
755: 			VertexInfo.Alpha0 = 1.0 - VertexInfo.Alpha0;
756: 		}
757: 	}
758: 	else if (const FRoadZoneSidewalk* LaneSidewalk = GetRoadZone().GetPtr<FRoadZoneSidewalk>())
759: 	{
760: 		VertexInfo.HeightOffset = LaneSidewalk->DefaultHeight;
761: 
762: 		const FRoadLaneAttributeSidewalkHeightValue DefHeightValue(VertexInfo.HeightOffset);
763: 
764: 		const auto& Lane = GetLane();
765: 
766: 		for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Lane.Attributes)
767: 		{
768: 			if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributeSidewalkHeightValue>())
769: 			{
770: 				VertexInfo.HeightOffset = Attribute.Evaluate(VertexInfo.Pos.SOffset - GetStartOffset(), DefHeightValue).Height;
771: 			}
772: 		}
773: 
774: 		for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Lane.Attributes)
775: 		{
776: 			if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
777: 			{
778: 				for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
779: 				{
780: 					if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
781: 					{
782: 						VertexInfo.HeightOffset = Value->GetHeightOffset(*this, Key.SOffset, Pos.SOffset, VertexInfo.Alpha0, VertexInfo.HeightOffset);
783: 					}
784: 				}
785: 			}
786: 		}
787: 	}
788: }

790: const TInstancedStruct<FRoadZone>& FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetRoadZone() const
791: { 
792: 	if (LaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
793: 	{
794: 		return GetLane().RoadZone;
795: 	}
796: 	else
797: 	{
798: 		static const TInstancedStruct<FRoadZone> Dummy;
799: 		return Dummy;
800: 	}
801: }
802: 
803: const TInstancedStruct<FRoadZone>& FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetRoadZoneAt(double SOffset) const
804: {
805: 	if (ZoneSpans.IsEmpty())
806: 	{
807: 		return GetRoadZone();
808: 	}
809: 
810: 	const int32 Index = MetaRoad::FindRoadLaneZoneSpan(ZoneSpans, SOffset);
811: 	return (Index != INDEX_NONE) ? ZoneSpans[Index].Zone : GetRoadZone();
812: }
813: 
814: const TInstancedStruct<FRoadZone>& FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetRoadZoneForTri(int TID) const
815: {
816: 	// The empty case is the norm; keeping it ahead of FindTri is what makes a lane without the attribute
817: 	// cost exactly what it did before (three map lookups per triangle per op is not free).
818: 	if (ZoneSpans.IsEmpty())
819: 	{
820: 		return GetRoadZone();
821: 	}
822: 
823: 	const FTriInfo TriInfo = FindTri(TID);
824: 	if (!TriInfo.IsValid())
825: 	{
826: 		return GetRoadZone();
827: 	}
828: 
829: 	// Mean S of the three corners. InsertZoneBoundaryEdges put a CONSTRAINED edge on every boundary, so no
830: 	// claimed triangle straddles one and the mean always lands in the span the whole triangle belongs to.
831: 	// GetSOffset (not Pos.SOffset) because a loop seam vertex reads as 0 or SplineLength depending on which
832: 	// side of the seam the triangle sits.
833: 	const double S = (GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.A->VID)
834: 		            + GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.B->VID)
835: 		            + GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.C->VID)) / 3.0;
836: 
837: 	return GetRoadZoneAt(S);
838: }
839: 
840: void FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetDistinctRoadZones(TArray<const TInstancedStruct<FRoadZone>*>& Out) const
841: {
842: 	Out.Reset();
---
746: 	VertexInfo.Alpha1 = bWithUV1 ? NormalizeLateral(VertexInfo.Pos.ROffset - SplineBounds.Max.Y, RoadWidth) : 0.0; // Alpha1 = [0..1]
747: 	VertexInfo.VID = VID;
748: 	VertexInfo.bLoopSeam = bLoopSeam;
749: 	VertexInfo.HeightOffset = 0;
750: 
751: 	if (const FRoadZoneDriving* LaneDriving = GetRoadZone().GetPtr<FRoadZoneDriving>())
752: 	{
753: 		if (LaneDriving && LaneDriving->bInvertUV0)
754: 		{
755: 			VertexInfo.Alpha0 = 1.0 - VertexInfo.Alpha0;
756: 		}
757: 	}
758: 	else if (const FRoadZoneSidewalk* LaneSidewalk = GetRoadZone().GetPtr<FRoadZoneSidewalk>())
759: 	{
760: 		VertexInfo.HeightOffset = LaneSidewalk->DefaultHeight;
761: 
762: 		const FRoadLaneAttributeSidewalkHeightValue DefHeightValue(VertexInfo.HeightOffset);
763: 
764: 		const auto& Lane = GetLane();
765: 
766: 		for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Lane.Attributes)
767: 		{
768: 			if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributeSidewalkHeightValue>())
769: 			{
770: 				VertexInfo.HeightOffset = Attribute.Evaluate(VertexInfo.Pos.SOffset - GetStartOffset(), DefHeightValue).Height;
771: 			}
772: 		}
773: 
774: 		for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Lane.Attributes)
775: 		{
776: 			if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
777: 			{
778: 				for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
779: 				{
780: 					if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
781: 					{
782: 						VertexInfo.HeightOffset = Value->GetHeightOffset(*this, Key.SOffset, Pos.SOffset, VertexInfo.Alpha0, VertexInfo.HeightOffset);
783: 					}
784: 				}
785: 			}
786: 		}
787: 	}
788: }

850: void FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::InsertZoneBoundaryEdges(FRoadArrangementBuilder& Builder, const TArray<FVector2D>& InsideLine2D, const TArray<FVector2D>& OutsideLine2D)
851: {
852: 	// Without a constrained edge at the boundary the Delaunay lets triangles straddle it, and a material /
853: 	// UV-density change would then follow a zigzag of triangle edges instead of a straight cross-section.
854: 	//
855: 	// GID = Other + PID = -1 is the curb-cut "rung" idiom (FRoadLaneAttributeCurbCutValue::OnPolygonCreated):
856: 	// an untracked edge never becomes a polygon boundary, so FindPolyline ignores it and this polygon still
857: 	// claims the triangles on BOTH sides -- the lane stays ONE polygon that merely gained an interior seam.
858: 	//
859: 	// Both endpoints are SNAPPED ONTO an existing boundary vertex rather than re-sampled, and the span is
860: 	// skipped when either side has none. Re-sampling GetRoadPosition() looks equivalent -- GetPolyline() put a
861: 	// point at exactly this S -- but is not: a sidewalk with cap curves calls RemovePointsFromEnd/Begin, which
862: 	// strip MaxValue * LaneWidth cm off each end of BOTH polylines, boundary points included. The outer end of
863: 	// such a span has no neighbouring lane to re-supply the vertex, so a re-sampled edge would introduce a
864: 	// vertex outside every polygon -- one with no FRoadVertexInfo, which fails the whole build at the height
865: 	// pass ("Mesh is broken", RoadBaseOp). Snapping also absorbs the case where NormalizePolyline kept a
866: 	// neighbouring sample instead of ours.
867: 	auto& RoadSplineCache = GetRoadSpline();
868: 
869: 	for (const FRoadLaneZoneSpan& Span : ZoneSpans)
870: 	{
871: 		// The first span may start at the lane start, where the begin cap already IS the cross edge.
872: 		if (Span.S0 - GetStartOffset() < GeomUtils::DefSnapDistance)
873: 		{
874: 			continue;
875: 		}
876: 
877: 		const FVector2D Inner{ RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, LaneIndex, 0.0, Span.S0, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World).Location };
878: 		const FVector2D Outer{ RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, LaneIndex, 1.0, Span.S0, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World).Location };
879: 
880: 		const int32 InnerIndex = FindPointOnLine2D(InsideLine2D, Inner);
881: 		const int32 OuterIndex = FindPointOnLine2D(OutsideLine2D, Outer);
882: 
883: 		if (InnerIndex == INDEX_NONE || OuterIndex == INDEX_NONE)
884: 		{
885: 			// Truncated away by a sidewalk cap (or dropped as a self-intersection). The zone still applies to
886: 			// the triangles there -- only the straight seam is lost, and the boundary follows triangle edges.
887: 			continue;
888: 		}
889: 
890: 		Builder.AddConstraintEdge(InsideLine2D[InnerIndex], OutsideLine2D[OuterIndex]);
891: 	}
892: }
893: 
894: FText FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetDescription() const
895: {
896: 	return FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane_GetDescription", "RoadLanePoly (ComponentName: \"{0}\", SectionIndex: {1}, LaneIndex: {2})"),
---
835: 		            + GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.C->VID)) / 3.0;
836: 
837: 	return GetRoadZoneAt(S);
838: }
839: 
840: void FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetDistinctRoadZones(TArray<const TInstancedStruct<FRoadZone>*>& Out) const
841: {
842: 	Out.Reset();
843: 	Out.Add(&GetRoadZone());
844: 	for (const FRoadLaneZoneSpan& Span : ZoneSpans)
845: 	{
846: 		Out.Add(&Span.Zone);
847: 	}
848: }
849: 
850: void FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::InsertZoneBoundaryEdges(FRoadArrangementBuilder& Builder, const TArray<FVector2D>& InsideLine2D, const TArray<FVector2D>& OutsideLine2D)
851: {

853: 	// UV-density change would then follow a zigzag of triangle edges instead of a straight cross-section.
854: 	//

857: 	// claims the triangles on BOTH sides -- the lane stays ONE polygon that merely gained an interior seam.
858: 	//

860: 	// skipped when either side has none. Re-sampling GetRoadPosition() looks equivalent -- GetPolyline() put a
861: 	// point at exactly this S -- but is not: a sidewalk with cap curves calls RemovePointsFromEnd/Begin, which
862: 	// strip MaxValue * LaneWidth cm off each end of BOTH polylines, boundary points included. The outer end of
863: 	// such a span has no neighbouring lane to re-supply the vertex, so a re-sampled edge would introduce a
864: 	// vertex outside every polygon -- one with no FRoadVertexInfo, which fails the whole build at the height
865: 	// pass ("Mesh is broken", RoadBaseOp). Snapping also absorbs the case where NormalizePolyline kept a
866: 	// neighbouring sample instead of ours.

870: 	{

872: 		if (Span.S0 - GetStartOffset() < GeomUtils::DefSnapDistance)
873: 		{
874: 			continue;
875: 		}
876: 
877: 		const FVector2D Inner{ RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, LaneIndex, 0.0, Span.S0, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World).Location };
878: 		const FVector2D Outer{ RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, LaneIndex, 1.0, Span.S0, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World).Location };
879: 
880: 		const int32 InnerIndex = FindPointOnLine2D(InsideLine2D, Inner);
881: 		const int32 OuterIndex = FindPointOnLine2D(OutsideLine2D, Outer);
882: 
883: 		if (InnerIndex == INDEX_NONE || OuterIndex == INDEX_NONE)
884: 		{

886: 			// the triangles there -- only the straight seam is lost, and the boundary follows triangle edges.
887: 			continue;
888: 		}

891: 	}
892: }

903: bool FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::SetUVLayers(FDynamicMesh3& Mesh, int TID) const
904: {
905: 	check(Mesh.Attributes()->NumUVLayers() >= 2);
906: 
907: 	const int UVMaxSize = Owner.Params.UVMaxSize;
908: 
909: 	const double SplineLength = GetSplineBounds().Max.X - GetSplineBounds().Min.X;
910: 
911: 	// Snap the tile count to a whole number so V closes on a CLOSED spline's seam (GetSOffset returns exactly
912: 	// SplineLength there, hence an integer V). Clamped to >= 1: RoundToInt is 0 whenever SplineLength < 0.5/Scale
913: 	// (under 200 cm for UV0, 500 cm for UV1) -- which collapsed the whole V axis, and Alpha1Mul with it, so a short
914: 	// junction connector sampled a single texel. The length guard covers empty/degenerate spline bounds.
915: 	auto Aligned = [SplineLength](double Scale)
916: 	{
917: 		return (SplineLength > UE_KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
918: 			? double(FMath::Max<int64>(1, FMath::RoundToInt(SplineLength * Scale))) / SplineLength
919: 			: Scale;
920: 	};
921: 
922: 	const double UV0ScaleFactorAligned = Aligned(Owner.Params.UV0ScaleFactor);
923: 	const double UV1ScaleFactorAligned = Aligned(Owner.Params.UV1ScaleFactor);
924: 
925: 	const double Alpha1Mul = GetSplineBounds().Extents().Y * UV1ScaleFactorAligned * 2.0;
926: 
927: 	auto TriInfo = FindTri(TID);
928: 
929: 	if (ensure(TriInfo.IsValid()))
930: 	{
931: 		const double SOffsetA = GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.A->VID);
932: 		const double SOffsetB = GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.B->VID);
933: 		const double SOffsetC = GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.C->VID);
934: 
935: 		// Resolved PER TRIANGLE: a RoadZone attribute key can change bOverrideUVDensity along S. With no
---
885: 			// Truncated away by a sidewalk cap (or dropped as a self-intersection). The zone still applies to
886: 			// the triangles there -- only the straight seam is lost, and the boundary follows triangle edges.
887: 			continue;
888: 		}
889: 
890: 		Builder.AddConstraintEdge(InsideLine2D[InnerIndex], OutsideLine2D[OuterIndex]);
891: 	}
892: }

897: 		FText::FromString(GetRoadSpline().GetOriginSplineName()),
898: 		FText::AsNumber(SectionIndex), 
899: 		FText::AsNumber(LaneIndex)
900: 	);

902: 
903: bool FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::SetUVLayers(FDynamicMesh3& Mesh, int TID) const
904: {
905: 	check(Mesh.Attributes()->NumUVLayers() >= 2);
906: 
907: 	const int UVMaxSize = Owner.Params.UVMaxSize;
908: 
909: 	const double SplineLength = GetSplineBounds().Max.X - GetSplineBounds().Min.X;
910: 
911: 	// Snap the tile count to a whole number so V closes on a CLOSED spline's seam (GetSOffset returns exactly
912: 	// SplineLength there, hence an integer V). Clamped to >= 1: RoundToInt is 0 whenever SplineLength < 0.5/Scale
913: 	// (under 200 cm for UV0, 500 cm for UV1) -- which collapsed the whole V axis, and Alpha1Mul with it, so a short
914: 	// junction connector sampled a single texel. The length guard covers empty/degenerate spline bounds.
915: 	auto Aligned = [SplineLength](double Scale)
916: 	{
917: 		return (SplineLength > UE_KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
918: 			? double(FMath::Max<int64>(1, FMath::RoundToInt(SplineLength * Scale))) / SplineLength
919: 			: Scale;
920: 	};
921: 
922: 	const double UV0ScaleFactorAligned = Aligned(Owner.Params.UV0ScaleFactor);
923: 	const double UV1ScaleFactorAligned = Aligned(Owner.Params.UV1ScaleFactor);
924: 
925: 	const double Alpha1Mul = GetSplineBounds().Extents().Y * UV1ScaleFactorAligned * 2.0;
926: 
927: 	auto TriInfo = FindTri(TID);
928: 
929: 	if (ensure(TriInfo.IsValid()))
930: 	{
931: 		const double SOffsetA = GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.A->VID);
932: 		const double SOffsetB = GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.B->VID);
933: 		const double SOffsetC = GetSOffset(TriInfo, TID, TriInfo.C->VID);
934: 
935: 		// Resolved PER TRIANGLE: a RoadZone attribute key can change bOverrideUVDensity along S. With no
936: 		// spans GetRoadZoneAt() returns the lane zone straight away, so this is the old whole-polygon value.
937: 		const double Density = ResolveUVDensity(GetRoadZoneAt((SOffsetA + SOffsetB + SOffsetC) / 3.0), Owner.Params.DefaultUVDensity);
938: 		// UV2 is the uniform-density layer. Tile snapping only buys a seamless wrap on a closed spline; on an open one
939: 		// it is pure density error (up to 0.5/N -- 25% at N=2), so it is applied only where it pays for itself.
940: 		const double UV2Factor = IsLoop() ? Aligned(Density) : Density;
941: 
942: 		if (auto* UVLayer0 = Mesh.Attributes()->GetUVLayer(0))
943: 		{
944: 			int MinV = (int)(FMath::Min3(SOffsetA, SOffsetB, SOffsetC) * UV0ScaleFactorAligned);
945: 			MinV =  MinV - MinV % UVMaxSize;
946: 
947: 			UVLayer0->SetTriangle(TID, FIndex3i{
948: 				UVLayer0->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.A->Alpha0, SOffsetA * UV0ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
949: 				UVLayer0->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.B->Alpha0, SOffsetB * UV0ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
950: 				UVLayer0->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.C->Alpha0, SOffsetC * UV0ScaleFactorAligned - MinV))
951: 			});
952: 		}
953: 
954: 		if (auto* UVLayer1 = Mesh.Attributes()->GetUVLayer(1))
955: 		{
956: 			int MinV = (int)(FMath::Min3(SOffsetA, SOffsetB, SOffsetC) * UV1ScaleFactorAligned);
957: 			MinV = MinV - MinV % UVMaxSize;
958: 
959: 			UVLayer1->SetTriangle(TID, FIndex3i{
960: 				UVLayer1->AppendElement(FVector2f((TriInfo.A->Alpha1 - 0.5) * Alpha1Mul + 0.5, SOffsetA * UV1ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
961: 				UVLayer1->AppendElement(FVector2f((TriInfo.B->Alpha1 - 0.5) * Alpha1Mul + 0.5, SOffsetB * UV1ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
962: 				UVLayer1->AppendElement(FVector2f((TriInfo.C->Alpha1 - 0.5) * Alpha1Mul + 0.5, SOffsetC * UV1ScaleFactorAligned - MinV))
963: 			});
964: 		}
965: 
966: 		if (auto* UVLayer2 = Mesh.Attributes()->GetUVLayer(2))
967: 		{
968: 			int MinV = (int)(FMath::Min3(SOffsetA, SOffsetB, SOffsetC) * UV2Factor);
969: 			MinV = MinV - MinV % UVMaxSize;
970: 
971: 			// U: centimetres from the SPLINE's reference line, so it runs continuously across lane boundaries.
972: 			// (It used to be Alpha2 -- centimetres from each LANE's inner edge, restarting at 0 on every lane,
973: 			// which cut the tiling mid-texel at every lane edge.)
974: 			UVLayer2->SetTriangle(TID, FIndex3i{
975: 				UVLayer2->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.A->Pos.ROffset * Density, SOffsetA * UV2Factor - MinV)),
976: 				UVLayer2->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.B->Pos.ROffset * Density, SOffsetB * UV2Factor - MinV)),
977: 				UVLayer2->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.C->Pos.ROffset * Density, SOffsetC * UV2Factor - MinV))
978: 			});
979: 		}
980: 
981: 		return true;
982: 	}
983: 
984: 	return false;
985: }

988: TArray<FRoadPosition> FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetPolyline(int InLaneIndex, double Alpha) const
989: {
990: 	auto& RoadSplineCache = GetRoadSpline();
991:

993: 	{
994: 		return {};
995: 	}

998:

1000: 	{
1001: 		return {};
1002: 	}

960: 				UVLayer1->AppendElement(FVector2f((TriInfo.A->Alpha1 - 0.5) * Alpha1Mul + 0.5, SOffsetA * UV1ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
961: 				UVLayer1->AppendElement(FVector2f((TriInfo.B->Alpha1 - 0.5) * Alpha1Mul + 0.5, SOffsetB * UV1ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
962: 				UVLayer1->AppendElement(FVector2f((TriInfo.C->Alpha1 - 0.5) * Alpha1Mul + 0.5, SOffsetC * UV1ScaleFactorAligned - MinV))

966: 		if (auto* UVLayer2 = Mesh.Attributes()->GetUVLayer(2))
967: 		{
968: 			int MinV = (int)(FMath::Min3(SOffsetA, SOffsetB, SOffsetC) * UV2Factor);
969: 			MinV = MinV - MinV % UVMaxSize;
970: 
971: 			// U: centimetres from the SPLINE's reference line, so it runs continuously across lane boundaries.
972: 			// (It used to be Alpha2 -- centimetres from each LANE's inner edge, restarting at 0 on every lane,
973: 			// which cut the tiling mid-texel at every lane edge.)
974: 			UVLayer2->SetTriangle(TID, FIndex3i{
975: 				UVLayer2->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.A->Pos.ROffset * Density, SOffsetA * UV2Factor - MinV)),
976: 				UVLayer2->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.B->Pos.ROffset * Density, SOffsetB * UV2Factor - MinV)),
977: 				UVLayer2->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.C->Pos.ROffset * Density, SOffsetC * UV2Factor - MinV))
978: 			});
979: 		}
980: 
981: 		return true;
982: 	}
983: 
984: 	return false;
985: }

988: TArray<FRoadPosition> FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetPolyline(int InLaneIndex, double Alpha) const
989: {
990: 	auto& RoadSplineCache = GetRoadSpline();
991: 
992: 	if (!RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections.IsValidIndex(SectionIndex))
993: 	{
994: 		return {};
995: 	}
996: 
997: 	const auto& Section = RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[SectionIndex];
998: 
999: 	if (InLaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex && !Section.CheckLaneIndex(InLaneIndex))
1000: 	{
1001: 		return {};
1002: 	}
1003: 
1004: 	TArray<FRoadPosition> OutPoints;
1005: 
1006: 	if (InLaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
1007: 	{
1008: 		const auto& Lane = Section.GetLaneByIndex(InLaneIndex);
1009: 
1010: 
1011: 		for (int i = Lane.GetStartSectionIndex(); i <= Lane.GetEndSectionIndex(); ++i)
1012: 		{
1013: 			TArray<FRoadPosition> TmpPoints;
1014: 			if (RoadSplineCache.ConvertSplineToPolyline(
1015: 				[this, InLaneIndex, &RoadSplineCache, Alpha](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
1016: 				{
1017: 					return RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
1018: 				},
1019: 				ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength,
1020: 				RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[i].SOffset, RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[i].SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1021: 				{}, true, TmpPoints))
1022: 			{
---
934: 
935: 		// Resolved PER TRIANGLE: a RoadZone attribute key can change bOverrideUVDensity along S. With no
936: 		// spans GetRoadZoneAt() returns the lane zone straight away, so this is the old whole-polygon value.
937: 		const double Density = ResolveUVDensity(GetRoadZoneAt((SOffsetA + SOffsetB + SOffsetC) / 3.0), Owner.Params.DefaultUVDensity);
938: 		// UV2 is the uniform-density layer. Tile snapping only buys a seamless wrap on a closed spline; on an open one
939: 		// it is pure density error (up to 0.5/N -- 25% at N=2), so it is applied only where it pays for itself.
940: 		const double UV2Factor = IsLoop() ? Aligned(Density) : Density;
941: 
942: 		if (auto* UVLayer0 = Mesh.Attributes()->GetUVLayer(0))
943: 		{
944: 			int MinV = (int)(FMath::Min3(SOffsetA, SOffsetB, SOffsetC) * UV0ScaleFactorAligned);
945: 			MinV =  MinV - MinV % UVMaxSize;
946: 
947: 			UVLayer0->SetTriangle(TID, FIndex3i{
948: 				UVLayer0->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.A->Alpha0, SOffsetA * UV0ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
949: 				UVLayer0->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.B->Alpha0, SOffsetB * UV0ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
950: 				UVLayer0->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.C->Alpha0, SOffsetC * UV0ScaleFactorAligned - MinV))
951: 			});
952: 		}
953: 
954: 		if (auto* UVLayer1 = Mesh.Attributes()->GetUVLayer(1))
955: 		{
956: 			int MinV = (int)(FMath::Min3(SOffsetA, SOffsetB, SOffsetC) * UV1ScaleFactorAligned);
957: 			MinV = MinV - MinV % UVMaxSize;
958: 
959: 			UVLayer1->SetTriangle(TID, FIndex3i{
960: 				UVLayer1->AppendElement(FVector2f((TriInfo.A->Alpha1 - 0.5) * Alpha1Mul + 0.5, SOffsetA * UV1ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
961: 				UVLayer1->AppendElement(FVector2f((TriInfo.B->Alpha1 - 0.5) * Alpha1Mul + 0.5, SOffsetB * UV1ScaleFactorAligned - MinV)),
962: 				UVLayer1->AppendElement(FVector2f((TriInfo.C->Alpha1 - 0.5) * Alpha1Mul + 0.5, SOffsetC * UV1ScaleFactorAligned - MinV))
963: 			});
964: 		}
965: 
966: 		if (auto* UVLayer2 = Mesh.Attributes()->GetUVLayer(2))
967: 		{
968: 			int MinV = (int)(FMath::Min3(SOffsetA, SOffsetB, SOffsetC) * UV2Factor);
969: 			MinV = MinV - MinV % UVMaxSize;
970: 
971: 			// U: centimetres from the SPLINE's reference line, so it runs continuously across lane boundaries.
972: 			// (It used to be Alpha2 -- centimetres from each LANE's inner edge, restarting at 0 on every lane,
973: 			// which cut the tiling mid-texel at every lane edge.)
974: 			UVLayer2->SetTriangle(TID, FIndex3i{
975: 				UVLayer2->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.A->Pos.ROffset * Density, SOffsetA * UV2Factor - MinV)),
976: 				UVLayer2->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.B->Pos.ROffset * Density, SOffsetB * UV2Factor - MinV)),
977: 				UVLayer2->AppendElement(FVector2f(TriInfo.C->Pos.ROffset * Density, SOffsetC * UV2Factor - MinV))
978: 			});
979: 		}
980: 
981: 		return true;
982: 	}
983: 
984: 	return false;
985: }

988: TArray<FRoadPosition> FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetPolyline(int InLaneIndex, double Alpha) const
989: {
990: 	auto& RoadSplineCache = GetRoadSpline();
991: 
992: 	if (!RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections.IsValidIndex(SectionIndex))
993: 	{
994: 		return {};
995: 	}
996: 
997: 	const auto& Section = RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[SectionIndex];
998: 
999: 	if (InLaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex && !Section.CheckLaneIndex(InLaneIndex))
1000: 	{
1001: 		return {};
1002: 	}
1003: 
1004: 	TArray<FRoadPosition> OutPoints;
1005: 
1006: 	if (InLaneIndex != MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex)
1007: 	{
1008: 		const auto& Lane = Section.GetLaneByIndex(InLaneIndex);
1009: 
1010: 
1011: 		for (int i = Lane.GetStartSectionIndex(); i <= Lane.GetEndSectionIndex(); ++i)
1012: 		{
1013: 			TArray<FRoadPosition> TmpPoints;
1014: 			if (RoadSplineCache.ConvertSplineToPolyline(
1015: 				[this, InLaneIndex, &RoadSplineCache, Alpha](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
1016: 				{
1017: 					return RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
1018: 				},
1019: 				ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength,
1020: 				RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[i].SOffset, RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[i].SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1021: 				{}, true, TmpPoints))
1022: 			{
1023: 				if (OutPoints.Num())
1024: 				{
1025: 					OutPoints.RemoveAt(OutPoints.Num() - 1);
1026: 				}
1027: 				OutPoints.Append(MoveTemp(TmpPoints));
1028: 			}
1029: 		}
1030: 
1031: 		if (Lane.RoadZone.GetPtr<FRoadZoneSidewalk>())
1032: 		{
1033: 			for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Lane.Attributes)
1034: 			{
1035: 				if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
1036: 				{
1037: 					for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
1038: 					{
1039: 						if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
1040: 						{
1041: 							TArray<double> Segments;
1042: 							Value->GetAdditionalSplinePoints(*this, Key.SOffset, Alpha, Segments);
1043: 
1044: 							TArray<FRoadPosition> AttributePoints;
1045: 							AttributePoints.Reserve(Segments.Num());
1046: 							for (double SOffset : Segments)
---
1013: 			TArray<FRoadPosition> TmpPoints;
1014: 			if (RoadSplineCache.ConvertSplineToPolyline(
1015: 				[this, InLaneIndex, &RoadSplineCache, Alpha](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
1016: 				{
1017: 					return RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
1018: 				},
1019: 				ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength,
1020: 				RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[i].SOffset, RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[i].SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1021: 				{}, true, TmpPoints))
1022: 			{
1023: 				if (OutPoints.Num())
1024: 				{
1025: 					OutPoints.RemoveAt(OutPoints.Num() - 1);
1026: 				}
1027: 				OutPoints.Append(MoveTemp(TmpPoints));
1028: 			}
1029: 		}
1030: 
1031: 		if (Lane.RoadZone.GetPtr<FRoadZoneSidewalk>())
1032: 		{
1033: 			for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Lane.Attributes)
1034: 			{
1035: 				if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
1036: 				{
1037: 					for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
1038: 					{
1039: 						if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
1040: 						{
1041: 							TArray<double> Segments;
1042: 							Value->GetAdditionalSplinePoints(*this, Key.SOffset, Alpha, Segments);
1043: 
1044: 							TArray<FRoadPosition> AttributePoints;
1045: 							AttributePoints.Reserve(Segments.Num());
1046: 							for (double SOffset : Segments)
1047: 							{
1048: 								AttributePoints.Add(RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World));
1049: 							}
1050: 							GeomUtils::InsertPolyline(OutPoints, AttributePoints);
1051: 						}
1052: 					}
1053: 				}
1054: 			}
1055: 		}
1056: 
1057: 		// Zone boundaries of the RoadZone attribute must be real vertices on this edge, so the constrained
1058: 		// edge InsertZoneBoundaryEdges lays across the lane meets the boundary AT a vertex instead of a
1059: 		// T-junction somewhere along a chord.
1060: 		//
1061: 		// Deliberately NOT gated by zone subtype (unlike the sidewalk-only block above): a RoadZone key is
1062: 		// legal on any lane. And deliberately keyed off `Lane` / `InLaneIndex` rather than this polygon's own
1063: 		// lane -- when the shared boundary between two lanes is assembled, this runs once for each of them
1064: 		// (see GetPolyline(ERoadLaneSide)), so both sides sample the same S and the seam welds.
1065: 		{
1066: 			TArray<double> BoundaryOffsets;
1067: 			MetaRoad::GetRoadLaneZoneBoundaryOffsets(Lane, Lane.GetStartOffset(), Lane.GetEndOffset(), BoundaryOffsets);
1068: 
1069: 			// Appended then normalized in one pass. NOT GeomUtils::InsertPolyline: with more than one point it
1070: 			// first RemoveSegmant()s everything between the first and last S, which would delete the sampled
1071: 			// curve between two boundaries.
1072: 			for (double BoundaryS : BoundaryOffsets)
1073: 			{
1074: 				OutPoints.Add(RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, BoundaryS, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World));
1075: 			}
1076: 
1077: 			if (BoundaryOffsets.Num())
1078: 			{
1079: 				GeomUtils::NormalizePolyline(OutPoints);
1080: 			}
1081: 		}
1082: 	}
1083: 	else
1084: 	{
1085: 		RoadSplineCache.ConvertSplineToPolyline(
1086: 			[this, &RoadSplineCache](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
1087: 			{
1088: 				return RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex, 0.0, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
1089: 			},
1090: 			ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength,
1091: 			Section.SOffset, Section.SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1092: 			{}, true, OutPoints);
1093: 
1094: 		// THE SAME points the looped zone's ring takes, INSERTED the same way. This is a second sampling of the
1095: 		// reference line -- the ring is the first -- and two densities for one curve is two chains that never
1096: 		// weld. Inserted rather than passed above, so one offset is one vertex on both hosts (see
1097: 		// InsertCentreLineRingCuts).
1098: 		TArray<TArray<double>> RingCuts;
1099: 		GatherCentreLineRingOffsets(RoadSplineCache, RoadSplineCache.GetSplineLength(), RingCuts);
1100: 		InsertCentreLineRingCuts(RoadSplineCache, SectionIndex, Section.SOffset, Section.SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1101: 			RingCuts, OutPoints);
1102: 	}
1103: 
1104: 	return OutPoints;
1105: }

1100: 		InsertCentreLineRingCuts(RoadSplineCache, SectionIndex, Section.SOffset, Section.SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1101: 			RingCuts, OutPoints);
1102: 	}
1103: 
1104: 	return OutPoints;
1105: }
1106: 
1107: TArray<FRoadPosition> FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetCenterPolyline(double S0, double S1) const
1108: {
1109: 	TArray<FRoadPosition> OutPoints;
1110: 
1111: 	auto& RoadSplineCache = GetRoadSpline();
1112: 	auto& Sections = RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections;
1113: 
1114: 	// SUSPECTED REDUNDANCY (verify at runtime before "fixing"): GetPolyline(0/±1, 0.0) below all sample the MEMBER
1115: 	// SectionIndex's S-range, NOT this loop's `Section`, so on an N-section road the loop appends N IDENTICAL copies
---
1017: 					return RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
1018: 				},
1019: 				ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength,
1020: 				RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[i].SOffset, RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections[i].SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1021: 				{}, true, TmpPoints))
1022: 			{
1023: 				if (OutPoints.Num())
1024: 				{
1025: 					OutPoints.RemoveAt(OutPoints.Num() - 1);
1026: 				}
1027: 				OutPoints.Append(MoveTemp(TmpPoints));
1028: 			}
1029: 		}
1030: 
1031: 		if (Lane.RoadZone.GetPtr<FRoadZoneSidewalk>())
1032: 		{
1033: 			for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Lane.Attributes)
1034: 			{
1035: 				if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
1036: 				{
1037: 					for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
1038: 					{
1039: 						if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
1040: 						{
1041: 							TArray<double> Segments;
1042: 							Value->GetAdditionalSplinePoints(*this, Key.SOffset, Alpha, Segments);
1043: 
1044: 							TArray<FRoadPosition> AttributePoints;
1045: 							AttributePoints.Reserve(Segments.Num());
1046: 							for (double SOffset : Segments)
1047: 							{
1048: 								AttributePoints.Add(RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World));
1049: 							}
1050: 							GeomUtils::InsertPolyline(OutPoints, AttributePoints);
1051: 						}
1052: 					}
1053: 				}
1054: 			}
1055: 		}
1056: 
1057: 		// Zone boundaries of the RoadZone attribute must be real vertices on this edge, so the constrained
1058: 		// edge InsertZoneBoundaryEdges lays across the lane meets the boundary AT a vertex instead of a
1059: 		// T-junction somewhere along a chord.
1060: 		//
1061: 		// Deliberately NOT gated by zone subtype (unlike the sidewalk-only block above): a RoadZone key is
1062: 		// legal on any lane. And deliberately keyed off `Lane` / `InLaneIndex` rather than this polygon's own
1063: 		// lane -- when the shared boundary between two lanes is assembled, this runs once for each of them
1064: 		// (see GetPolyline(ERoadLaneSide)), so both sides sample the same S and the seam welds.
1065: 		{
1066: 			TArray<double> BoundaryOffsets;
1067: 			MetaRoad::GetRoadLaneZoneBoundaryOffsets(Lane, Lane.GetStartOffset(), Lane.GetEndOffset(), BoundaryOffsets);
1068: 
1069: 			// Appended then normalized in one pass. NOT GeomUtils::InsertPolyline: with more than one point it
1070: 			// first RemoveSegmant()s everything between the first and last S, which would delete the sampled
1071: 			// curve between two boundaries.
1072: 			for (double BoundaryS : BoundaryOffsets)
1073: 			{
1074: 				OutPoints.Add(RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, BoundaryS, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World));
1075: 			}
1076: 
1077: 			if (BoundaryOffsets.Num())
1078: 			{
1079: 				GeomUtils::NormalizePolyline(OutPoints);
1080: 			}
1081: 		}
1082: 	}
1083: 	else
1084: 	{
1085: 		RoadSplineCache.ConvertSplineToPolyline(
1086: 			[this, &RoadSplineCache](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
1087: 			{
1088: 				return RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex, 0.0, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
1089: 			},
1090: 			ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength,
1091: 			Section.SOffset, Section.SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1092: 			{}, true, OutPoints);
1093: 
1094: 		// THE SAME points the looped zone's ring takes, INSERTED the same way. This is a second sampling of the
1095: 		// reference line -- the ring is the first -- and two densities for one curve is two chains that never
1096: 		// weld. Inserted rather than passed above, so one offset is one vertex on both hosts (see
1097: 		// InsertCentreLineRingCuts).
1098: 		TArray<TArray<double>> RingCuts;
1099: 		GatherCentreLineRingOffsets(RoadSplineCache, RoadSplineCache.GetSplineLength(), RingCuts);
1100: 		InsertCentreLineRingCuts(RoadSplineCache, SectionIndex, Section.SOffset, Section.SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1101: 			RingCuts, OutPoints);
1102: 	}
1103: 
1104: 	return OutPoints;
1105: }

1060: 		//
1061: 		// Deliberately NOT gated by zone subtype (unlike the sidewalk-only block above): a RoadZone key is
1062: 		// legal on any lane. And deliberately keyed off `Lane` / `InLaneIndex` rather than this polygon's own
1063: 		// lane -- when the shared boundary between two lanes is assembled, this runs once for each of them
1064: 		// (see GetPolyline(ERoadLaneSide)), so both sides sample the same S and the seam welds.
1065: 		{
1066: 			TArray<double> BoundaryOffsets;
1067: 			MetaRoad::GetRoadLaneZoneBoundaryOffsets(Lane, Lane.GetStartOffset(), Lane.GetEndOffset(), BoundaryOffsets);
1068: 
1069: 			// Appended then normalized in one pass. NOT GeomUtils::InsertPolyline: with more than one point it
1070: 			// first RemoveSegmant()s everything between the first and last S, which would delete the sampled
1071: 			// curve between two boundaries.
1072: 			for (double BoundaryS : BoundaryOffsets)
1073: 			{
1074: 				OutPoints.Add(RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, BoundaryS, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World));
1075: 			}
1076: 
1077: 			if (BoundaryOffsets.Num())
1078: 			{
1079: 				GeomUtils::NormalizePolyline(OutPoints);
1080: 			}
1081: 		}
1082: 	}
1083: 	else
1084: 	{
1085: 		RoadSplineCache.ConvertSplineToPolyline(
1086: 			[this, &RoadSplineCache](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
1087: 			{
1088: 				return RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex, 0.0, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
1089: 			},
1090: 			ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength,
1091: 			Section.SOffset, Section.SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1092: 			{}, true, OutPoints);
1093: 
1094: 		// THE SAME points the looped zone's ring takes, INSERTED the same way. This is a second sampling of the
1095: 		// reference line -- the ring is the first -- and two densities for one curve is two chains that never
1096: 		// weld. Inserted rather than passed above, so one offset is one vertex on both hosts (see
1097: 		// InsertCentreLineRingCuts).
1098: 		TArray<TArray<double>> RingCuts;
1099: 		GatherCentreLineRingOffsets(RoadSplineCache, RoadSplineCache.GetSplineLength(), RingCuts);
1100: 		InsertCentreLineRingCuts(RoadSplineCache, SectionIndex, Section.SOffset, Section.SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1101: 			RingCuts, OutPoints);
1102: 	}
1103: 
1104: 	return OutPoints;
1105: }
1106: 
1107: TArray<FRoadPosition> FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetCenterPolyline(double S0, double S1) const
1108: {
1109: 	TArray<FRoadPosition> OutPoints;
1110: 
1111: 	auto& RoadSplineCache = GetRoadSpline();
1112: 	auto& Sections = RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections;
1113: 
1114: 	// SUSPECTED REDUNDANCY (verify at runtime before "fixing"): GetPolyline(0/±1, 0.0) below all sample the MEMBER
1115: 	// SectionIndex's S-range, NOT this loop's `Section`, so on an N-section road the loop appends N IDENTICAL copies
1116: 	// (NormalizePolyline + GetSubPolyline then clip to [S0,S1]). Likely wasteful, possibly harmless (arrangement
1117: 	// dedup), possibly a multi-section boundary defect. Needs a baked multi-section road to confirm before changing.
1118: 	for (auto& Section: Sections)
1119: 	{
1120: 		OutPoints.Append(GetPolyline(0, 0.0));
1121: 
1122: 		if(Section.Left.Num())
1123: 		{
1124: 			OutPoints.Append(GetPolyline(-1, 0.0));
1125: 		}
1126: 
1127: 		if (Section.Right.Num())
1128: 		{
---
1030: 
1031: 		if (Lane.RoadZone.GetPtr<FRoadZoneSidewalk>())
1032: 		{
1033: 			for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Lane.Attributes)
1034: 			{
1035: 				if (Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
1036: 				{
1037: 					for (auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
1038: 					{
1039: 						if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr< FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
1040: 						{
1041: 							TArray<double> Segments;
1042: 							Value->GetAdditionalSplinePoints(*this, Key.SOffset, Alpha, Segments);
1043: 
1044: 							TArray<FRoadPosition> AttributePoints;
1045: 							AttributePoints.Reserve(Segments.Num());
1046: 							for (double SOffset : Segments)
1047: 							{
1048: 								AttributePoints.Add(RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World));
1049: 							}
1050: 							GeomUtils::InsertPolyline(OutPoints, AttributePoints);
1051: 						}
1052: 					}
1053: 				}
1054: 			}
1055: 		}
1056: 
1057: 		// Zone boundaries of the RoadZone attribute must be real vertices on this edge, so the constrained
1058: 		// edge InsertZoneBoundaryEdges lays across the lane meets the boundary AT a vertex instead of a
1059: 		// T-junction somewhere along a chord.
1060: 		//
1061: 		// Deliberately NOT gated by zone subtype (unlike the sidewalk-only block above): a RoadZone key is
1062: 		// legal on any lane. And deliberately keyed off `Lane` / `InLaneIndex` rather than this polygon's own
1063: 		// lane -- when the shared boundary between two lanes is assembled, this runs once for each of them
1064: 		// (see GetPolyline(ERoadLaneSide)), so both sides sample the same S and the seam welds.
1065: 		{
1066: 			TArray<double> BoundaryOffsets;
1067: 			MetaRoad::GetRoadLaneZoneBoundaryOffsets(Lane, Lane.GetStartOffset(), Lane.GetEndOffset(), BoundaryOffsets);
1068: 
1069: 			// Appended then normalized in one pass. NOT GeomUtils::InsertPolyline: with more than one point it
1070: 			// first RemoveSegmant()s everything between the first and last S, which would delete the sampled
1071: 			// curve between two boundaries.
1072: 			for (double BoundaryS : BoundaryOffsets)
1073: 			{
1074: 				OutPoints.Add(RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, InLaneIndex, Alpha, BoundaryS, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World));
1075: 			}
1076: 
1077: 			if (BoundaryOffsets.Num())
1078: 			{
1079: 				GeomUtils::NormalizePolyline(OutPoints);
1080: 			}
1081: 		}
1082: 	}
1083: 	else
1084: 	{
1085: 		RoadSplineCache.ConvertSplineToPolyline(
1086: 			[this, &RoadSplineCache](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
1087: 			{
1088: 				return RoadSplineCache.GetRoadPosition(SectionIndex, MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex, 0.0, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
1089: 			},
1090: 			ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength,
1091: 			Section.SOffset, Section.SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1092: 			{}, true, OutPoints);
1093: 
1094: 		// THE SAME points the looped zone's ring takes, INSERTED the same way. This is a second sampling of the
1095: 		// reference line -- the ring is the first -- and two densities for one curve is two chains that never
1096: 		// weld. Inserted rather than passed above, so one offset is one vertex on both hosts (see
1097: 		// InsertCentreLineRingCuts).
1098: 		TArray<TArray<double>> RingCuts;
1099: 		GatherCentreLineRingOffsets(RoadSplineCache, RoadSplineCache.GetSplineLength(), RingCuts);
1100: 		InsertCentreLineRingCuts(RoadSplineCache, SectionIndex, Section.SOffset, Section.SOffsetEnd_Cached,
1101: 			RingCuts, OutPoints);
1102: 	}
1103: 
1104: 	return OutPoints;
1105: }

1107: TArray<FRoadPosition> FProceduralPolygon_RoadLane::GetCenterPolyline(double S0, double S1) const
1108: {
1109: 	TArray<FRoadPosition> OutPoints;
1110: 
1111: 	auto& RoadSplineCache = GetRoadSpline();
1112: 	auto& Sections = RoadSplineCache.RoadLayout.Sections;
1113: 
1114: 	// SUSPECTED REDUNDANCY (verify at runtime before "fixing"): GetPolyline(0/±1, 0.0) below all sample the MEMBER
1115: 	// SectionIndex's S-range, NOT this loop's `Section`, so on an N-section road the loop appends N IDENTICAL copies
1116: 	// (NormalizePolyline + GetSubPolyline then clip to [S0,S1]). Likely wasteful, possibly harmless (arrangement
1117: 	// dedup), possibly a multi-section boundary defect. Needs a baked multi-section road to confirm before changing.
1118: 	for (auto& Section: Sections)
1119: 	{
1120: 		OutPoints.Append(GetPolyline(0, 0.0));
1121: 
1122: 		if(Section.Left.Num())
1123: 		{
1124: 			OutPoints.Append(GetPolyline(-1, 0.0));
1125: 		}
1126: 
1127: 		if (Section.Right.Num())
1128: 		{
1129: 			OutPoints.Append(GetPolyline(+1, 0.0));
1130: 		}
1131: 	}
1132: 
1133: 	GeomUtils::NormalizePolyline(OutPoints);
1134: 	OutPoints = GeomUtils::GetSubPolyline(OutPoints, S0, S1);
1135: 
1136: 	return OutPoints;
1137: }

17: #include "Utils/CurveUtils.h"

19: #include "Algo/MaxElement.h"
20: #include "Algo/MinElement.h"

26: #define LOCTEXT_NAMESPACE "FProceduralPolygon"
27: 
28: using namespace MetaRoad;
29: #include "RoadMeshBuild/Polygon/RoadPolygonCommon.h"
30: 
31: void FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop::ForEachRingCustomization(TFunctionRef<void(const FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue&, double AbsS)> Fn) const
32: {
33: 	const auto& Sections = GetRoadSpline().RoadLayout.Sections;
34: 	for (const FRoadLaneSection& Section : Sections)
35: 	{
36: 		for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Section.Attributes)
37: 		{
38: 			if (!Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
39: 			{
40: 				continue;
41: 			}
42: 			for (const auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
43: 			{
44: 				if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
45: 				{
46: 					// Centre-line keys are SECTION-relative (unlike the ring, which spans the whole spline).
47: 					Fn(*Value, Section.SOffset + Key.SOffset);
48: 				}
49: 			}
50: 		}
51: 	}
52: }
53: 
54: FVector2D FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop::SampleRingInward(double S, double InwardDistance) const
55: {
56: 	const auto& Spline = GetRoadSpline();
57: 	// The ring is NOT at R = 0: GetRoadPosition(_, ZeroLaneIndex, _) yields EvalLaneROffset(0) == 0 PLUS the
58: 	// layout's ROffset curve. Anchor the inward offset on that same R or the contour drifts off the boundary
59: 	// wherever ROffset has keys.
60: 	const double RingR = Spline.EvalROffset(S);
61: 	return FVector2D(Spline.GetRoadPosition(S, RingR + InwardSignR * InwardDistance, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World).Location);
62: }
63: 
64: FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop::FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop(const FRoadTriangulationData& Owner, FRoadArrangementBuilder& Builder, int SplineIndex, double MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, double MinSegmentLength)
65: 	:FProceduralPolygon_RoadBase(Owner, SplineIndex)
66: {
67: 	ResultInfo = { EGeometryResultType::InProgress };
68: 
69: 	auto RoadPositionFunc = [this](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
70: 	{
71: 		return GetRoadSpline().GetRoadPosition(0, MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex, 0.0, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
72: 	};
73: 
74: 	RingLength = GetRoadSpline().SplineCurves.GetSplineLength();
75: 
76: 	TArray<FRoadPosition> RoadPoints;
77: 	if (!GetRoadSpline().ConvertSplineToPolyline(RoadPositionFunc, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength, 0.0, RingLength, {}, true, RoadPoints))
78: 	{
79: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop_ConvertSplineToPolyline", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop: {0}: Can't ConvertSplineToPolyline"), GetDescription()));
80: 		return;
81: 	}
82: 
83: 	// The cut's profile breakpoints, INSERTED rather than handed to the sampler as segment boundaries: without a
84: 	// vertex at each one the ramp has no topology to bend over, but a boundary would also make the sampler
85: 	// subdivide every interval between them and double the ring's density for the same MeshDensity. The identical
86: 	// list goes into lane +-1's copy of this same reference line -- see GatherCentreLineRingOffsets.
87: 	{
88: 		TArray<TArray<double>> RingCuts;
89: 		GatherCentreLineRingOffsets(GetRoadSpline(), RingLength, RingCuts);
90: 		InsertCentreLineRingCuts(GetRoadSpline(), 0, 0.0, RingLength, RingCuts, RoadPoints);
91: 	}

32: {

34: 	for (const FRoadLaneSection& Section : Sections)

47: 					Fn(*Value, Section.SOffset + Key.SOffset);
48: 				}
49: 			}
50: 		}
51: 	}

10: #include "MetaRoadSettings.h"
11: #include "Assets/RoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomization.h"
12: #include "Assets/RoadLaneAttributeSidewalkHeight.h"
13: #if METAROAD_PRO
14: #include "Assets/RoadLaneAttributePolygon.h"
15: #endif
16: #include "Utils/OpUtils.h"
17: #include "Utils/CurveUtils.h"
18: #include "RoadMeshBuild/Ops/TriangulateRoadOp.h"
19: #include "Algo/MaxElement.h"
20: #include "Algo/MinElement.h"
21: #include "Misc/Optional.h"
22: #include "Utils/RoadUtils.h"
23: #include "Utils/GeomUtils.h"
24: #include "RoadMeshBuild/PolylineRecovery.h" // FindPolyline (the phase-1 PID-chain recovery)
25: 
26: #define LOCTEXT_NAMESPACE "FProceduralPolygon"
27: 
28: using namespace MetaRoad;
29: #include "RoadMeshBuild/Polygon/RoadPolygonCommon.h"
30: 
31: void FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop::ForEachRingCustomization(TFunctionRef<void(const FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue&, double AbsS)> Fn) const
32: {
33: 	const auto& Sections = GetRoadSpline().RoadLayout.Sections;
34: 	for (const FRoadLaneSection& Section : Sections)
35: 	{
36: 		for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Section.Attributes)
37: 		{
38: 			if (!Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
39: 			{
40: 				continue;
41: 			}
42: 			for (const auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
43: 			{
44: 				if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
45: 				{
46: 					// Centre-line keys are SECTION-relative (unlike the ring, which spans the whole spline).
47: 					Fn(*Value, Section.SOffset + Key.SOffset);
48: 				}
49: 			}
50: 		}
51: 	}
52: }
53: 
54: FVector2D FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop::SampleRingInward(double S, double InwardDistance) const
55: {
56: 	const auto& Spline = GetRoadSpline();
57: 	// The ring is NOT at R = 0: GetRoadPosition(_, ZeroLaneIndex, _) yields EvalLaneROffset(0) == 0 PLUS the
58: 	// layout's ROffset curve. Anchor the inward offset on that same R or the contour drifts off the boundary
59: 	// wherever ROffset has keys.
60: 	const double RingR = Spline.EvalROffset(S);
61: 	return FVector2D(Spline.GetRoadPosition(S, RingR + InwardSignR * InwardDistance, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World).Location);
62: }
63: 
64: FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop::FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop(const FRoadTriangulationData& Owner, FRoadArrangementBuilder& Builder, int SplineIndex, double MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, double MinSegmentLength)
65: 	:FProceduralPolygon_RoadBase(Owner, SplineIndex)
66: {
67: 	ResultInfo = { EGeometryResultType::InProgress };
68: 
69: 	auto RoadPositionFunc = [this](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
70: 	{
71: 		return GetRoadSpline().GetRoadPosition(0, MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex, 0.0, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
72: 	};
73: 
74: 	RingLength = GetRoadSpline().SplineCurves.GetSplineLength();
75: 
76: 	TArray<FRoadPosition> RoadPoints;
77: 	if (!GetRoadSpline().ConvertSplineToPolyline(RoadPositionFunc, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength, 0.0, RingLength, {}, true, RoadPoints))
78: 	{
79: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop_ConvertSplineToPolyline", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop: {0}: Can't ConvertSplineToPolyline"), GetDescription()));
80: 		return;
81: 	}
82: 
83: 	// The cut's profile breakpoints, INSERTED rather than handed to the sampler as segment boundaries: without a
84: 	// vertex at each one the ramp has no topology to bend over, but a boundary would also make the sampler
85: 	// subdivide every interval between them and double the ring's density for the same MeshDensity. The identical
86: 	// list goes into lane +-1's copy of this same reference line -- see GatherCentreLineRingOffsets.
87: 	{
88: 		TArray<TArray<double>> RingCuts;
89: 		GatherCentreLineRingOffsets(GetRoadSpline(), RingLength, RingCuts);
90: 		InsertCentreLineRingCuts(GetRoadSpline(), 0, 0.0, RingLength, RingCuts, RoadPoints);
91: 	}
92: 
93: 	TArray<FVector2D> Points2D;
94: 	Points2D.Reserve(RoadPoints.Num());
---

15: #endif

32: {

34: 	for (const FRoadLaneSection& Section : Sections)
35: 	{
36: 		for (const auto& [Desc, Attribute] : Section.Attributes)
37: 		{
38: 			if (!Attribute.IsChildOf<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
39: 			{
40: 				continue;
41: 			}
42: 			for (const auto& Key : Attribute.Keys)
43: 			{
44: 				if (auto* Value = Key.GetValuePtr<FRoadLaneAttributePolygonCustomizationValue>())
45: 				{
46: 					// Centre-line keys are SECTION-relative (unlike the ring, which spans the whole spline).
47: 					Fn(*Value, Section.SOffset + Key.SOffset);
48: 				}
49: 			}
50: 		}
51: 	}
52: }

54: FVector2D FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop::SampleRingInward(double S, double InwardDistance) const
55: {
56: 	const auto& Spline = GetRoadSpline();
57: 	// The ring is NOT at R = 0: GetRoadPosition(_, ZeroLaneIndex, _) yields EvalLaneROffset(0) == 0 PLUS the
58: 	// layout's ROffset curve. Anchor the inward offset on that same R or the contour drifts off the boundary
59: 	// wherever ROffset has keys.
60: 	const double RingR = Spline.EvalROffset(S);
61: 	return FVector2D(Spline.GetRoadPosition(S, RingR + InwardSignR * InwardDistance, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World).Location);
62: }
63: 
64: FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop::FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop(const FRoadTriangulationData& Owner, FRoadArrangementBuilder& Builder, int SplineIndex, double MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, double MinSegmentLength)
65: 	:FProceduralPolygon_RoadBase(Owner, SplineIndex)
66: {
67: 	ResultInfo = { EGeometryResultType::InProgress };
68: 
69: 	auto RoadPositionFunc = [this](double SOffset, ESplineCoordinateSpace::Type CoordinateSpace)
70: 	{
71: 		return GetRoadSpline().GetRoadPosition(0, MetaRoad::ZeroLaneIndex, 0.0, SOffset, CoordinateSpace);
72: 	};
73: 
74: 	RingLength = GetRoadSpline().SplineCurves.GetSplineLength();
75: 
76: 	TArray<FRoadPosition> RoadPoints;
77: 	if (!GetRoadSpline().ConvertSplineToPolyline(RoadPositionFunc, ESplineCoordinateSpace::World, MaxSquareDistanceFromSpline, MinSegmentLength, 0.0, RingLength, {}, true, RoadPoints))
78: 	{
79: 		ResultInfo.SetFailed(FText::Format(LOCTEXT("FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop_ConvertSplineToPolyline", "FProceduralPolygon_RoadLoop: {0}: Can't ConvertSplineToPolyline"), GetDescription()));
80: 		return;
81: 	}
82: 
83: 	// The cut's profile breakpoints, INSERTED rather than handed to the sampler as segment boundaries: without a
84: 	// vertex at each one the ramp has no topology to bend over, but a boundary would also make the sampler
85: 	// subdivide every interval between them and double the ring's density for the same MeshDensity. The identical
86: 	// list goes into lane +-1's copy of this same reference line -- see GatherCentreLineRingOffsets.
87: 	{
88: 		TArray<TArray<double>> RingCuts;
89: 		GatherCentreLineRingOffsets(GetRoadSpline(), RingLength, RingCuts);
90: 		InsertCentreLineRingCuts(GetRoadSpline(), 0, 0.0, RingLength, RingCuts, RoadPoints);
91: 	}
92: 
93: 	TArray<FVector2D> Points2D;
94: 	Points2D.Reserve(RoadPoints.Num());
95: 	for (auto& It : RoadPoints)
96: 	{
97: 		Points2D.Add(FVector2D{ It.Location });
98: 	}
99: 	GeomUtils::RemovedPolylineSelfIntersection(Points2D);
100: 
101: 	// Which R direction points into the island — from the ONE shared helper, which decides it by probing the ring
102: 	// rather than by reasoning about winding. Shared deliberately: the viewport preview of a cut resolves the same
103: 	// question through the same call, and two independent derivations of one sign is how a cut ends up drawn into
104: 	// the island and carved out of it (or the reverse).
105: 	InwardSignR = MetaRoad::GetLoopInwardSignR(GetRoadSpline().GetOriginSpline());
106: 
107: 	int GID = SelectSurfaceGID(GetRoadZone());
108: 
109: 	// KNOWN EXCEPTION, named on the builder rather than collapsed: the loop below runs i < Num-1 with no wrap
110: 	// (i.e. it assumes the last point duplicates the first), while the winding computation twenty lines above
111: 	// runs (i + 1) % Num (i.e. it assumes the opposite). Both cannot be right, and it also feeds no sample
---

80: 		return;
81: 	}

83: 	// The cut's profile breakpoints, INSERTED rather than handed to the sampler as segment boundaries: without a
84: 	// vertex at each one the ramp has no topology to bend over, but a boundary would also make the sampler

90: 		InsertCentreLineRingCuts(GetRoadSpline(), 0, 0.0, RingLength, RingCuts, RoadPoints);
91: 	}
92: 
93: 	TArray<FVector2D> Points2D;
94: 	Points2D.Reserve(RoadPoints.Num());
95: 	for (auto& It : RoadPoints)
96: 	{
97: 		Points2D.Add(FVector2D{ It.Location });
98: 	}
99: 	GeomUtils::RemovedPolylineSelfIntersection(Points2D);

101: 	// Which R direction points into the island — from the ONE shared helper, which decides it by probing the ring

105: 	InwardSignR = MetaRoad::GetLoopInwardSignR(GetRoadSpline().GetOriginSpline());
106: 
107: 	int GID = SelectSurfaceGID(GetRoadZone());
108: 
109: 	// KNOWN EXCEPTION, named on the builder rather than collapsed: the loop below runs i < Num-1 with no wrap
110: 	// (i.e. it assumes the last point duplicates the first), while the winding computation twenty lines above
111: 	// runs (i + 1) % Num (i.e. it assumes the opposite). Both cannot be right, and it also feeds no sample
112: 	// digest -- so collapsing it into InsertPolyline would move the fingerprint's `samples` column on every road
113: 	// with a looped zone, i.e. break the instrument that would check the collapse. See the header.
114: 	LineInfo = Builder.InsertRingAsPolyline(Points2D, GID);

116: 	if (!LineInfo.IsValid())
